While the armies of the Zandalari Empire and their Horde allies raced back to Dazar'alor, Rastakhan was ultimately confronted and mortally wounded by the Alliance. As Talanji approached her dying father, he apologized to his daughter before he siphoned the power of <a href="/wiki/Bwonsamdi" title="Bwonsamdi">Bwonsamdi</a> into her.
</p><p>Sometime after the death of her father, Talanji created a new Zanchuli Council consisting of <a href="/wiki/Wardruid_Loti" title="Wardruid Loti">Wardruid Loti</a>, <a href="/wiki/Hexlord_Raal" title="Hexlord Raal">Hexlord Raal</a>, <a href="/wiki/Jo%27nok,_Bulwark_of_Torcali" title="Jo&#39;nok, Bulwark of Torcali">Jo'nok, Bulwark of Torcali</a>, <a href="/wiki/High_Prelate_Rata" title="High Prelate Rata">High Prelate Rata</a>, <a href="/wiki/Rakera" title="Rakera">General Rakera</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Lashk" title="Lashk">Lashk</a>, to help lead the Zandalari Empire.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-62" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-62"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>62<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> As she prepared to undergo the rite of queens and kings, the Horde leadership was among those in attendance.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-63" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-63"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>63<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> However, Zandalari dissidents, outraged and fearful that worshippers of Bwonsamdi could rule the Zandalari Empire, led by <a href="/wiki/Samara" title="Samara">the White Widow</a>, rose in a small-scale rebellion. Talanji and her allies dealt with the protests by making the dissenters see reason and allowed them to bend the knee,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-64" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-64"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>64<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-65" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-65"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>65<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> however not all chose to accept and instead fought to the death for their opinions.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-66" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-66"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>66<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-67" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-67"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>67<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Following the White Widow's death, Talanji passed the various loa trials and emerged as the Queen of the Zandalari Empire. <sup id="cite&#95;ref-68" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-68"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>68<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Recognizing that the Horde sacrificed for the Zandalari and vice versa, Talanji declared that only a pledge of loyalty would honor that commitment. Thus, the Zandalari joined the Horde, as its equals.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-69" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-69"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>69<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> As part of the Horde, Talanji selected <a href="/wiki/Zandalari_troll_(playable)" title="Zandalari troll (playable)">worthy champions</a> to represent the might of the Zandalari Empire.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-70" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-70"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>70<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> After they proved the might of the Zandalari, Talanji called them to return <a href="/wiki/Dazar%27alor" title="Dazar&#39;alor">Dazar'alor</a> where she blessed them with the <a href="/wiki/Heritage_of_the_Zandalari" title="Heritage of the Zandalari">Heritage of the Zandalari</a>, encouraged them to wear it with pride, and declared them among the greatest champions of the Zandalari.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-71" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-71"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>71<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>When <a href="/wiki/Varok_Saurfang" title="Varok Saurfang">Varok Saurfang</a> and <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a> called their <a href="/wiki/Adventurer" title="Adventurer">loyalists</a> for <a href="/wiki/Horde_War_Campaign" title="Horde War Campaign">battle</a>, Talanji was the only Horde leader not to pick a side and the armies of the Zandalari Empire neither aided Saurfang's revolutionaries nor <a href="/wiki/Banshee_loyalists" title="Banshee loyalists">Sylvanas's loyalists</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-72" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-72"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>72<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-73" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-73"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>73<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Shadowlands">Shadowlands</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Zandalari_Empire?action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Shadowlands"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><span class="icon sl-inline"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Shadowlands" title="Shadowlands"><img alt="Shadowlands" src="/images/thumb/Shadowlands-Icon-Inline.png/48px-Shadowlands-Icon-Inline.png?d84a63" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="48" height="17" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a></span></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Shadowlands" title="World of Warcraft: Shadowlands">Shadowlands</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>Under the <a href="/wiki/Lich_King" title="Lich King">Lich King</a> <a href="/wiki/Bolvar_Fordragon" title="Bolvar Fordragon">Bolvar Fordragon</a>'s command, the <a href="/wiki/Four_Horsemen" title="Four Horsemen">Four Horsemen</a> acquired fallen champions of both the <a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde">Horde</a> and <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a> through the battlefields and graveyards of <a href="/wiki/Azeroth" title="Azeroth">Azeroth</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-74" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-74"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>74<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> including the Zandalari. Thus, the <a href="/wiki/Knights_of_the_Ebon_Blade" title="Knights of the Ebon Blade">Knights of the Ebon Blade</a> welcomed a new <a href="/wiki/Allied_race" title="Allied race">generation</a> of <a href="/wiki/Death_knight" title="Death knight">death knights</a> in preparation for <a href="/wiki/War_against_the_Jailer" title="War against the Jailer">times ahead</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-75" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-75"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>75<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> They were later sent by <a href="/wiki/Highlord" title="Highlord">Highlord</a> <a href="/wiki/Darion_Mograine" title="Darion Mograine">Darion Mograine</a> to <a href="/wiki/Orgrimmar" title="Orgrimmar">Orgrimmar</a>, in order to join the Horde once more and help protect the world.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-76" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-76"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>76<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Shadows_Rising">Shadows Rising</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Zandalari_Empire?action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Shadows Rising"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/Novels" title="Novels"><img alt="WoW-novel-logo-16x62.png" src="/images/WoW-novel-logo-16x62.png?6916a4" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="62" height="16" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="62" data-file-height="16" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to the <i>Warcraft</i> <a href="/wiki/Novels" title="Novels">novels</a>, <a href="/wiki/Novellas" title="Novellas">novellas</a>, or <a href="/wiki/Short_stories" title="Short stories">short stories</a>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>Following the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a>, Talanji's rule was threatened by the <a href="/wiki/Widow%27s_Bite" title="Widow&#39;s Bite">Widow's Bite</a> led by her childhood friend <a href="/wiki/Apari" title="Apari">Apari</a>. Not approving of the <a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde">Horde</a> and <a href="/wiki/Bwonsamdi" title="Bwonsamdi">Bwonsamdi</a>, the rebels spread false rumors, made attempts on the new queen's life, attacked Dazar'alor, and kidnapped noblemen. Although the Horde wanted to help Talanji, they also needed to know what was happening in Zandalar, so they elevated <a href="/wiki/Zekhan" title="Zekhan">Zekhan</a> to the rank of Horde <a href="/wiki/Ambassador" title="Ambassador">ambassador</a> and was then sent to Zuldazar. Although Talanji's forces were bigger, they could not apprehend the rebels in time, being spread across the swamps. Seeking the death of Bwonsamdi, the rebels allied with <a href="/wiki/Banshee_loyalists" title="Banshee loyalists">Sylvanas' loyalists</a> and worked on dismantling his shrines.
</p><p>Ultimately, Horde forces arrived to Zandalar and helped Talanji and the Zandalari forces to defeat the rebels as well as the undead loyalists led by <a href="/wiki/Nathanos_Blightcaller" title="Nathanos Blightcaller">Nathanos Blightcaller</a>. This was also achieved with the help of <a href="/wiki/Tayo" title="Tayo">Tayo</a>, a former rebel who started disapproving with their methods when Apari wanted to sacrifice children, who provided the Zandalari and Horde crucial information about traps laid by Nathanos. This victory brought the Zandalari Empire closer to the Horde, with Talanji taking her seat at the <a href="/wiki/Horde_Council" title="Horde Council">Horde Council</a>, and Tayo becoming the empire's ambassador to the Horde.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-77" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-77"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>77<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="The_War_Within">The War Within</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Zandalari_Empire?action=edit&amp;section=21" title="Edit section: The War Within"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><span class="icon tww-inline"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_The_War_Within" title="The War Within"><img alt="The War Within" src="/images/thumb/TheWarWithin-Petopia-Logo.png/40px-TheWarWithin-Petopia-Logo.png?c7c8d5" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="22" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="100" data-file-height="54" /></a></span></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_The_War_Within" title="World of Warcraft: The War Within">The War Within</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>The Zandalari were amongst the <a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde">Horde</a> forces that arrived to <a href="/wiki/Khaz_Algar" title="Khaz Algar">Khaz Algar</a> for the fight against <a href="/wiki/Xal%27atath" title="Xal&#39;atath">Xal'atath</a>. While <a href="/wiki/Captain_Rez%27okun" title="Captain Rez&#39;okun">Captain Rez'okun</a> remained with the fleet and <a href="/wiki/General_Rakera" class="mw-redirect" title="General Rakera">General Rakera</a> aided in training the <a href="/wiki/Earthen" title="Earthen">earthen</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-78" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-78"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>78<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/High_Prelate_Rata" title="High Prelate Rata">High Prelate Rata</a> and her <a href="/wiki/Zandalari_prelates" title="Zandalari prelates">Zandalari prelates</a> were amongst the forces that arrived to aid the <a href="/wiki/Arathi" title="Arathi">Arathi</a> when the <a href="/wiki/Nerubian" title="Nerubian">nerubians</a> of <a href="/wiki/Azj-Kahet" title="Azj-Kahet">Azj-Kahet</a> launched an all-out assault on <a href="/wiki/Hallowfall" title="Hallowfall">Hallowfall</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-79" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-79"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>79<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>At some point, <a href="/wiki/Monte_Gazlowe" title="Monte Gazlowe">Monte Gazlowe</a> worked out a deal with the empire to allow his cartel to mine <a href="/wiki/Kaja%27mite" title="Kaja&#39;mite">Kaja'mite</a> near the western coast of <a href="/wiki/Zuldazar" title="Zuldazar">Zuldazar</a>. However, <a href="/wiki/Jastor_Gallywix" title="Jastor Gallywix">Jastor Gallywix</a> seized control of the operation and destroyed a mountain sacred to <a href="/wiki/Pa%27ku" title="Pa&#39;ku">Pa'ku</a>, angering the Zandalari. <a href="/wiki/Hexlord_Raal" title="Hexlord Raal">Hexlord Raal</a> and some <a href="/wiki/Paku%27ai" title="Paku&#39;ai">Paku'ai</a> scouts arrived to investigate, and while he understood that it was not the <a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Cartel" title="Bilgewater Cartel">Bilgewater Cartel</a>'s fault, he expected Gazlowe to fix things.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-80" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-80"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>80<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>

<p>Zandalari are also seen worshiping <a href="/wiki/Zim%27Torga" title="Zim&#39;Torga">Zim'Torga</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-83" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-83"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>83<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Dubra%27Jin" title="Dubra&#39;Jin">Dubra'Jin</a><sup id="cite&#95;ref-84" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-84"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>84<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and <a href="/wiki/Ksu%27la" title="Ksu&#39;la">Ksu'la</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-85" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-85"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>85<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> though none of those beings was called a loa. While the Matron of the <a href="/wiki/Lun%27alai" title="Lun&#39;alai">Lun'alai</a> is outright not considered a loa by some zandalari.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-86" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-86"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>86<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Speculation">Speculation</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Zandalari_Empire?action=edit&amp;section=27" title="Edit section: Speculation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<table style="background: transparent;">

<tbody><tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/File:Questionmark-medium.png" class="image"><img alt="Questionmark-medium.png" src="/images/Questionmark-medium.png?4f580e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="41" height="55" data-file-width="41" data-file-height="55" /></a>
</td>
<td valign="top"><div style="border-top: 1px solid #808080; border-bottom: 1px solid #808080; padding: 1ex 1em; margin-top: 0.3ex; max-width: 34em;"><small style="line-height: 120%;">This article or section includes speculation, observations or opinions possibly supported by lore or by Blizzard officials. <b>It should not be taken as representing official lore.</b></small></div>
</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>The "final defeat from evil within their own tribe" that was mentioned by the Zandalari at Yojamba's isle<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Confessor&#39;sBindings&#95;33-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Confessor&#39;sBindings-33"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> may allude to the summoning of Hakkar on Zandalar and the subsequent release of the <a href="/wiki/Plague_of_corrupted_blood" title="Plague of corrupted blood">blood plague</a> and the creation of the <a href="/wiki/Blood_troll" title="Blood troll">blood trolls</a>. This led to the Zandalari "banishing the trolls who would destroy their own kind", which would correspond to the trolls who summoned the blood god. However, Hakkar was summoned to Zandalar "many millennia ago", which would be an odd choice of words if it was the same incident as this one which occurred 1,500 years ago.
</p>
<ul><li>The mention that <a href="/wiki/Confessor" title="Confessor">confessors</a> not only "keep them in tune with the spirits, but they also preserve the flesh" may allude to the <a href="/wiki/Blood_magic" title="Blood magic">blood magic</a> employed by the blood trolls, which has been described as "corrupting the creatures around them and turning them into blood beasts".<sup id="cite&#95;ref-87" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-87"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>87<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul>
